\begin{split}&\langle{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}),\eta_{U}\rangle+\langle{\mathbf{B}}^{-1}{\rm grad}_{{\mathbf{B}}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}){\mathbf{B}}^{-1},\eta_{B}\rangle+\langle{\rm grad}_{{\mathbf{V}}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}),\eta_{V}\rangle\\
=&\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}^{r}({\rm grad}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}),\eta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})})={\rm D}\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})[\eta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}]\\
=&\langle\nabla_{{\mathbf{U}}}\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}),\eta_{U}\rangle+\langle\nabla_{{\mathbf{B}}}\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}),\eta_{B}\rangle+\langle\nabla_{{\mathbf{V}}}\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}),\eta_{V}\rangle\\
=&\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}{\mathbf{B}},\eta_{U}\rangle+\langle{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}},\eta_{B}\rangle\\
&+\langle(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}},\eta_{V}\rangle,\quad\forall\eta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}\in T_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r}^{q_{2}},\end{split}

(95)

and

\begin{split}&\quad\langle{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}){\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}},\eta_{U}\rangle+\langle{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{Y}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}){\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}},\eta_{Y}\rangle\\
&=\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}^{r}({\rm grad}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}),\eta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})})={\rm D}\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})[\eta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}]\\
&=\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{U}}\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}),\eta_{U}\rangle+\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{Y}}\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}),\eta_{Y}\rangle\\
&=\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}},\eta_{U}\rangle+\langle(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}},\eta_{Y}\rangle,\quad\forall\eta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}\in T_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r}^{q_{3}}.\end{split}

(96)

From (
94
) and (
95
), we have

\begin{split}{\rm grad}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})&=\begin{bmatrix}\nabla f({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}){\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}\\
(\nabla f({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}\end{bmatrix},\\
{\rm grad}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})&=\begin{bmatrix}P_{T_{\mathbf{U}}{\rm St}(r,p_{1})}\left(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}{\mathbf{B}}\right)\\
{\mathbf{B}}{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}){\mathbf{B}}\\
P_{T_{\mathbf{V}}{\rm St}(r,p_{2})}\left((\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\right)\end{bmatrix}.\end{split}

(97)

Moreover, from (
96
), we know
{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{Y}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})=(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}
. Suppose
{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})={\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}{\mathbf{D}}+{\mathbf{U}}\boldsymbol{\Omega}\in T_{\mathbf{U}}{\rm St}(r,p_{1})
. Then from (
96
), we have
P_{T_{\mathbf{U}}{\rm St}(r,p_{1})}({\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}){\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}-\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}})=0
. This yields
{\mathbf{D}}={\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}
and
\boldsymbol{\Omega}
is uniquely determined by the Sylvester equation:
{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{\top}+\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{\top}{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}={\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}-{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}}
. So

{\rm grad}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})=\begin{bmatrix}P_{{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}+{\mathbf{U}}\boldsymbol{\Omega}\\
(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}.

By Lemma
7
,
\mathcal{V}_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r}^{q_{1}}
is orthogonal to
\mathcal{H}_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r}^{q_{1}}
with respect to
\bar{g}^{r}_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}
, we have
\overline{{\rm grad}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}={\rm grad}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})
by Lemma
1
.

Next, we compute
\overline{{\rm grad}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}
. Given
\eta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}=[({\mathbf{U}}\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{1}+{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}{\mathbf{D}}_{1})^{\top}\quad{\mathbf{S}}\quad({\mathbf{V}}\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{2}+{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}{\mathbf{D}}_{2})^{\top}]^{\top}\in T_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r}^{q_{2}}
, and suppose
P_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}^{\mathcal{H}}(\eta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})})=[({\mathbf{U}}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Omega}}+{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}\widetilde{{\mathbf{D}}}_{1})^{\top}\quad\widetilde{{\mathbf{S}}}\quad(-{\mathbf{V}}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Omega}}+{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}\widetilde{{\mathbf{D}}}_{2})^{\top}]\in\mathcal{H}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r}^{q_{2}}
. By definition

\begin{split}&(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Omega}},\widetilde{{\mathbf{S}}},\widetilde{{\mathbf{D}}}_{1},\widetilde{{\mathbf{D}}}_{2})\\
=&\mathop{\rm arg\min}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{\prime}=-\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r},{\mathbf{S}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{S}^{r\times r},\\
{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{(p_{1}-r)\times r},{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{(p_{2}-r)\times r}\end{subarray}}\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}^{r}\left(\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{U}}(\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{1}-\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{\prime})+{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}({\mathbf{D}}_{1}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime})\\
{\mathbf{S}}-{\mathbf{S}}^{\prime}\\
{\mathbf{V}}(\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{2}+\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{\prime})+{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}({\mathbf{D}}_{2}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})\end{bmatrix},\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{U}}(\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{1}-\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{\prime})+{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}({\mathbf{D}}_{1}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime})\\
{\mathbf{S}}-{\mathbf{S}}^{\prime}\\
{\mathbf{V}}(\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{2}+\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{\prime})+{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}({\mathbf{D}}_{2}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})\end{bmatrix}\right)\\
=&\mathop{\rm arg\min}_{\begin{subarray}{c}\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{\prime}=-\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{{}^{\prime}\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r},{\mathbf{S}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{S}^{r\times r},\\
{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{(p_{1}-r)\times r},{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{(p_{2}-r)\times r}\end{subarray}}\|\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{1}-\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{\prime}\|^{2}_{\rm F}+\|\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{2}+\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{\prime}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|{\mathbf{D}}_{i}-{\mathbf{D}}_{i}^{\prime}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\|{\mathbf{B}}^{-1/2}({\mathbf{S}}-{\mathbf{S}}^{\prime}){\mathbf{B}}^{-1/2}\|_{\rm F}^{2}.\end{split}

So we have
(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Omega}},\widetilde{{\mathbf{S}}},\widetilde{{\mathbf{D}}}_{1},\widetilde{{\mathbf{D}}}_{2})=((\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{1}-\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{2})/2,{\mathbf{S}},{\mathbf{D}}_{1},{\mathbf{D}}_{2})
, i.e.,

P_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}^{\mathcal{H}}(\eta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})})=\begin{bmatrix}P_{{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}}\eta_{U}+{\mathbf{U}}({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\eta_{U}-{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\eta_{V})/2\\
\eta_{B}\\
P_{{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}}\eta_{V}-{\mathbf{V}}({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\eta_{U}-{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\eta_{V})/2\end{bmatrix},\quad\eta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}\in T_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r}^{q_{2}}.

(98)

Then recall the definition of the projection operator
P_{T_{\mathbf{U}}{\rm St}(r,p_{1})}
from Table
1
, we have

\begin{split}\overline{{\rm grad}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}\overset{\eqref{eq: quotient-gradient-general}}{=}&P_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}^{\mathcal{H}}\left({\rm grad}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})\right)=\begin{bmatrix}P_{{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}{\mathbf{B}}+{\mathbf{U}}\boldsymbol{\Omega}\\
{\mathbf{B}}{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}){\mathbf{B}}\\
P_{{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}}(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}-{\mathbf{V}}\boldsymbol{\Omega}\end{bmatrix},\end{split}

where

\begin{split}\boldsymbol{\Omega}&=\left({\rm Skew}\left({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}{\mathbf{B}}\right)-{\rm Skew}\left({\mathbf{V}}^{\top}(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\right)\right)/2\\
&={\rm Skew}({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}){\mathbf{B}}/2+{\mathbf{B}}{\rm Skew}({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}})/2.\end{split}

Finally,

\begin{split}\overline{{\rm grad}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}\overset{\eqref{eq: quotient-gradient-general}}{=}P_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{V}})}^{\mathcal{H}}({\rm grad}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}))&=\begin{bmatrix}P_{{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}}\left(P_{{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}+{\mathbf{U}}\boldsymbol{\Omega}\right)\\
(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}\\
&=\begin{bmatrix}P_{{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{V}}^{-1}_{\mathbf{Y}}\\
(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}.\end{split}

Step 2.
Suppose
{\mathbf{X}}
has has SVD
{\mathbf{U}}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\prime}{\mathbf{V}}^{{}^{\prime}\top}
with
{\mathbf{U}}={\mathbf{U}}^{\prime}{\mathbf{O}}_{1},{\mathbf{V}}={\mathbf{V}}^{\prime}{\mathbf{O}}_{2}
(
{\mathbf{O}}_{1},{\mathbf{O}}_{2}\in\mathbb{O}_{r}
). Then for

\begin{split}\xi_{\mathbf{X}}&=[{\mathbf{U}}\quad{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}]\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{S}}&{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\top}\\
{\mathbf{D}}_{1}&{\mathbf{0}}\end{bmatrix}[{\mathbf{V}}\quad{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}]^{\top}=[{\mathbf{U}}^{\prime}\quad{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}]\begin{bmatrix}{\mathbf{O}}_{1}{\mathbf{S}}{\mathbf{O}}_{2}^{\top}&{\mathbf{O}}_{1}{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\top}\\
{\mathbf{D}}_{1}{\mathbf{O}}_{2}^{\top}&{\mathbf{0}}\end{bmatrix}[{\mathbf{V}}^{\prime}\quad{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}]^{\top},\end{split}

by
[
LLZ21
, Proposition 2]
we have in the general case

\begin{split}{\rm Hess}f({\mathbf{X}})[\xi_{\mathbf{X}},\xi_{\mathbf{X}}]&=\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{X}})[\xi_{\mathbf{X}},\xi_{\mathbf{X}}]+2\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{X}}),{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}{\mathbf{O}}_{2}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{{}^{\prime}-1}{\mathbf{O}}_{1}{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\top}{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}^{\top}\rangle\\
&=\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{X}})[\xi_{\mathbf{X}},\xi_{\mathbf{X}}]+2\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{X}}),{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1}{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\top}{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}^{\top}\rangle,\end{split}

where
\boldsymbol{\Sigma}={\mathbf{O}}_{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\prime}{\mathbf{O}}_{2}={\mathbf{U}}^{\top}{\mathbf{X}}{\mathbf{V}}
.

Step 3.
For convenience, we denote
\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{L}}}^{r}(\eta_{L},\theta_{L})={\rm tr}({\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}\eta_{L}^{\top}\theta_{L})
as the
{\mathbf{L}}
part inner product of
\bar{g}^{r}_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}
and
\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{r}(\eta_{R},\theta_{R})={\rm tr}({\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}\eta_{R}^{\top}\theta_{R})
as the
{\mathbf{R}}
part inner product of
\bar{g}^{r}_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}
. First following similar arguments as in (
76
) and (
78
), for three vector fields
\eta,\theta,\xi
on
\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r}^{q_{1}}
, we have

\begin{split}{\rm D}\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}(\eta,\theta)[\xi]={\rm tr}({\rm D}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}[\xi]\eta_{L}^{\top}\theta_{L})+{\rm tr}({\rm D}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}[\xi]\eta_{R}^{\top}\theta_{R})+\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}({\rm D}\eta[\xi],\theta)+\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}(\eta,{\rm D}\theta[\xi])\end{split}

and

\begin{split}2\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}(\widebar{\nabla}_{\xi}\eta,\theta)&=2\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}({\rm D}\eta[\xi],\theta)+{\rm tr}({\rm D}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}[\xi]\eta_{L}^{\top}\theta_{L})+{\rm tr}({\rm D}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}[\xi]\eta_{R}^{\top}\theta_{R})+{\rm tr}({\rm D}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}[\eta]\xi_{L}^{\top}\theta_{L})\\
&\quad+{\rm tr}({\rm D}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}[\eta]\xi_{R}^{\top}\theta_{R})-{\rm tr}({\rm D}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}[\theta]\eta_{L}^{\top}\xi_{L})-{\rm tr}({\rm D}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}[\theta]\eta_{R}^{\top}\xi_{R}).\end{split}

(99)

Then

\begin{split}&\quad\overline{{\rm Hess}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})},\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}]\\
&=\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}^{r}\left(\overline{{\rm Hess}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])[\theta_{[{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}]}]},\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}\right)\\
&\overset{\eqref{eq: quotient-hessian-linear-from}}{=}\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}^{r}\left(P_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}^{\mathcal{H}}\left(\widebar{\nabla}_{\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}}\overline{{\rm grad}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}\right),\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}\right)\\
&\overset{\text{Lemma }\ref {lm: quotient-hessian-riemannian-connection-equal}}{=}\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}^{r}\left(\widebar{\nabla}_{\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}}\overline{{\rm grad}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])},\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}\right)\\
&\overset{\eqref{eq: koszul-formula-gLR}}{=}\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{L}}}^{r}\left({\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{{\mathbf{L}}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}],\theta_{L}\right)+\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{R}}}^{r}\left({\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{{\mathbf{R}}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}],\theta_{R}\right)\\
&\quad+{\rm tr}({\rm D}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}[\overline{{\rm grad}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}]\theta_{L}^{\top}\theta_{L})/2+{\rm tr}({\rm D}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}[\overline{{\rm grad}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}]\theta_{R}^{\top}\theta_{R})/2.\end{split}

(100)

Next, we compute
{\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{{\mathbf{L}}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}]
.

\begin{split}&{\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{{\mathbf{L}}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}]\\
=&\lim_{t\to 0}\left(\nabla f\left(({\mathbf{L}}+t\theta_{L})({\mathbf{R}}+t\theta_{R})^{\top}\right)({\mathbf{R}}+t\theta_{R}){\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}}+t\theta_{L},{\mathbf{R}}+t\theta_{R}}^{-1}-\nabla f({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}){\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}\right)/t\\
=&\nabla f({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\theta_{R}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}+\nabla f({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}){\mathbf{R}}{\rm D}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}[\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}]+\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})[{\mathbf{L}}\theta_{R}^{\top}+\theta_{L}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}.\end{split}

(101)

Similarly we have

\begin{split}&{\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{{\mathbf{R}}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}]\\
=&\left(\nabla f({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\right)^{\top}\theta_{L}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}+\left(\nabla f({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{L}}{\rm D}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}[\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}]+\left(\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})[{\mathbf{L}}\theta_{R}^{\top}+\theta_{L}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}.\end{split}

(102)

Plugging (
101
) and (
102
) into (
100
), we have

\begin{split}&\quad\overline{{\rm Hess}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})},\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}]\\
&=\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})[{\mathbf{L}}\theta_{R}^{\top}+\theta_{L}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top},{\mathbf{L}}\theta_{R}^{\top}+\theta_{L}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}]+2\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}),\theta_{L}\theta_{R}^{\top}\rangle\\
&\quad+\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}){\mathbf{R}}{\rm D}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}[\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}],\theta_{L}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}\rangle+\langle\left(\nabla f({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top})\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{L}}{\rm D}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}^{-1}[\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}],\theta_{R}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}\rangle\\
&\quad+{\rm tr}({\rm D}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}[\overline{{\rm grad}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}]\theta_{L}^{\top}\theta_{L})/2+{\rm tr}({\rm D}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}[\overline{{\rm grad}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}]\theta_{R}^{\top}\theta_{R})/2.\end{split}

Step 4.
For convenience, we denote
\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{U}}}^{r}(\eta_{U},\theta_{U})={\rm tr}(\eta_{U}^{\top}\theta_{U})
as the
{\mathbf{U}}
part inner product of
\bar{g}^{r}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}
,
\bar{g}^{r}_{{\mathbf{B}}}(\eta_{B},\theta_{B})={\rm tr}({\mathbf{B}}^{-1}\eta_{B}{\mathbf{B}}^{-1}\theta_{B})
as the
{\mathbf{B}}
part inner product of
\bar{g}^{r}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}
and
\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{V}}}^{r}(\eta_{V},\theta_{V})={\rm tr}(\eta_{V}^{\top}\theta_{V})
as the
{\mathbf{V}}
part inner product of
\bar{g}^{r}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}
. Then

\begin{split}&\quad\overline{{\rm Hess}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})},\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}]\\
&=\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}^{r}\left(\overline{{\rm Hess}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])[\theta_{[{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}]}]},\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}\right)\\
&\overset{\eqref{eq: quotient-hessian-linear-from},\text{Lemma }\ref {lm: quotient-hessian-riemannian-connection-equal}}{=}\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}^{r}\left(\widebar{\nabla}_{\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}}\overline{{\rm grad}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])},\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}\right)\\
&\overset{\eqref{eq: koszul-formula-simple-manifolds}}{=}\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{U}}}^{r}\left({\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}],\theta_{U}\right)+\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{B}}}^{r}\left({\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{B}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}],\theta_{B}\right)\\
&\quad-{\rm tr}\left({\mathbf{B}}^{-1}{\rm Sym}\left(\theta_{B}{\mathbf{B}}^{-1}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{B}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}\right){\mathbf{B}}^{-1}\theta_{B}\right)+\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{V}}}^{r}\left({\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}],\theta_{V}\right).\end{split}

(103)

Recall the horizontal projection operator
P_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}^{\mathcal{H}}
given in (
98
), and let
P_{{\mathbf{U}}}^{\mathcal{H}}
,
P_{{\mathbf{B}}}^{\mathcal{H}}
and
P_{{\mathbf{V}}}^{\mathcal{H}}
be the restriction of
P_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}^{\mathcal{H}}
to the
{\mathbf{U}}
,
{\mathbf{B}}
and
{\mathbf{V}}
components, respectively. Moreover, define
{\mathbf{U}}_{t}={\mathbf{U}}+t\theta_{U},{\mathbf{V}}_{t}={\mathbf{V}}+t\theta_{V},{\mathbf{B}}_{t}={\mathbf{B}}+t\theta_{B}
. By (
97
), we have

\begin{split}\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{U}:=&\lim_{t\to 0}\left({\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}}_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{t},{\mathbf{V}}_{t})-{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}))\right)/t\\
=&P_{T_{\mathbf{U}}{\rm St}(r,p_{1})}\left(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})\theta_{V}{\mathbf{B}}+\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}\theta_{B}+\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})[\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{B}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top}]{\mathbf{V}}{\mathbf{B}}\right)\\
&-2{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{U}}\theta_{U}^{\top})\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}{\mathbf{B}}+\theta_{U}{\rm Skew}\left({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}{\mathbf{B}}\right)+{\mathbf{U}}{\rm Skew}\left(\theta_{U}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}{\mathbf{B}}\right);\\
\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{V}:=&\lim_{t\to 0}\left({\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}}_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{t},{\mathbf{V}}_{t})-{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}))\right)/t\\
=&P_{T_{\mathbf{V}}{\rm St}(r,p_{2})}\Big(\left(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})\right)^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}+\left(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{B}\\
&+\left(\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})[\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{B}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top}]\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\Big)-2{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{V}}\theta_{V}^{\top})\left(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\\
&+\theta_{V}{\rm Skew}\left({\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\left(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\right)+{\mathbf{V}}{\rm Skew}\left(\theta_{V}^{\top}\left(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\right).\end{split}

(104)

Then

\begin{split}&{\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}]\\
=&\lim_{t\to 0}\Big(P_{{\mathbf{U}}_{t}}^{\mathcal{H}}({\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}}_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{t},{\mathbf{V}}_{t}))-P_{\mathbf{U}}^{\mathcal{H}}({\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}))\Big)/t\\
=&P_{\mathbf{U}}^{\mathcal{H}}\left(\lim_{t\to 0}\left({\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}}_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{t},{\mathbf{V}}_{t})-{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})\right)/t\right)-{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{U}}\theta_{U}^{\top}){\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})\\
&-(\theta_{U}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{V}^{\top}){\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})/2\\
\overset{\eqref{eq: directional-derivative-gradient-UBV}}{=}&P_{\mathbf{U}}^{\mathcal{H}}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{U})-{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{U}}\theta_{U}^{\top}){\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})-(\theta_{U}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{V}^{\top}){\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})/2,\end{split}

(105)

and

\begin{split}&{\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}]\\
=&\lim_{t\to 0}\Big(P_{{\mathbf{V}}_{t}}^{\mathcal{H}}({\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}}_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{t},{\mathbf{V}}_{t}))-P_{\mathbf{V}}^{\mathcal{H}}({\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}))\Big)/t\\
=&P_{\mathbf{V}}^{\mathcal{H}}\left(\lim_{t\to 0}\left({\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}}_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{t},{\mathbf{V}}_{t})-{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})\right)/t\right)-{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{V}}\theta_{V}^{\top}){\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})\\
&-(\theta_{V}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{V}}\theta_{U}^{\top}){\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})/2\\
\overset{\eqref{eq: directional-derivative-gradient-UBV}}{=}&P_{\mathbf{U}}^{\mathcal{H}}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{V})-{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{V}}\theta_{V}^{\top}){\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})-(\theta_{V}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{V}}\theta_{U}^{\top}){\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})/2.\end{split}

(106)

In addition

\begin{split}&{\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{B}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}]\\
=&\lim_{t\to 0}\Big({\rm grad}_{\mathbf{B}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}}_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{t},{\mathbf{V}}_{t})-{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{B}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})\Big)/t\\
=&{\mathbf{B}}\Big({\rm Sym}\left(\theta_{U}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}\right)+{\rm Sym}\left({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})\theta_{V}\right)\\
&+{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})[\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{B}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top}]{\mathbf{V}}\Big){\mathbf{B}}\\
&+{\mathbf{B}}{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}})\theta_{B}+\theta_{B}{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}){\mathbf{B}}.\end{split}

(107)

Let
\boldsymbol{\Delta}={\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}
,
\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime}=\theta_{U}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}){\mathbf{V}}
and
\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime\prime}={\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})\theta_{V}
. By (
105
), (
106
) and (
107
) and observing the fact
\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}\in\mathcal{H}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r}^{q_{2}}
, we have

\begin{split}&\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{U}}}^{r}\left({\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}],\theta_{U}\right)\\
=&\left\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{U}-{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{U}}\theta_{U}^{\top}){\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})-(\theta_{U}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{V}^{\top}){\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,\bar{h}_{r}({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})/2,\theta_{U}\right\rangle\\
=&\left\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{U},\theta_{U}\right\rangle+\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta},{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}){\mathbf{B}}+{\mathbf{B}}{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\theta_{V}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U})\rangle/2-\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime},{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}\rangle/2-\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime\prime},{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{U}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}\rangle/2;\\
\overset{\eqref{eq: directional-derivative-gradient-UBV}}{=}&\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})[\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{B}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top},\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}]+\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}),\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top}\rangle\\
&-\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta},\theta_{U}^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}\rangle+\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime},2\theta_{B}-{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}-\theta_{U}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\rangle/2+\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta},{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}){\mathbf{B}}+{\mathbf{B}}{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\theta_{V}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U})\rangle/2\\
&-\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime},{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}\rangle/2-\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime\prime},{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{U}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}\rangle/2\\
=&\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})[\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{B}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top},\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}]+\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}),\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top}\rangle\\
&+\left\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta},{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}){\mathbf{B}}+{\mathbf{B}}{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\theta_{V}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U})-2\theta_{U}^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}\right\rangle/2\\
&+\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime},\theta_{B}-{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}-\theta_{U}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}/2\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime\prime},{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{U}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}\rangle/2,\end{split}

(108)

and similarly

\begin{split}&\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{V}}}^{r}\left({\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}],\theta_{V}\right)\\
=&\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})[\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{B}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top},{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top}]+\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}),\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top}\rangle\\
&+\left\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta},{\mathbf{B}}{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\theta_{V}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\theta_{V})+{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\theta_{V}){\mathbf{B}}-2{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top}\theta_{V}\right\rangle/2\\
&+\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime\prime},\theta_{B}-{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top}{\mathbf{V}}-{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\theta_{V}/2\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime},{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\theta_{V}{\mathbf{B}}\rangle/2,\end{split}

(109)

and

\begin{split}&\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{B}}}^{r}\left({\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{B}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}],\theta_{B}\right)-{\rm tr}\left({\mathbf{B}}^{-1}{\rm Sym}\left(\theta_{B}{\mathbf{B}}^{-1}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{B}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}\right){\mathbf{B}}^{-1}\theta_{B}\right)\\
=&\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})[\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{B}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top},{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{B}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}]+2\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta},\theta_{B}{\mathbf{B}}^{-1}\theta_{B}\rangle\\
&+\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime},\theta_{B}\rangle+\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime\prime},\theta_{B}\rangle-\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta},\theta_{B}{\mathbf{B}}^{-1}\theta_{B}\rangle\\
=&\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})[\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{B}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top},{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{B}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}]+\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta},\theta_{B}{\mathbf{B}}^{-1}\theta_{B}\rangle+\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime},\theta_{B}\rangle+\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime\prime},\theta_{B}\rangle.\end{split}

(110)

By plugging (
108
), (
109
) and (
110
) into (
103
), we finally have

\begin{split}&\overline{{\rm Hess}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})},\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}]\\
=&\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{U}}}^{r}\left({\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}],\theta_{U}\right)+\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{B}}}^{r}\left({\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{B}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}],\theta_{B}\right)\\
&-{\rm tr}\left({\mathbf{B}}^{-1}{\rm Sym}\left(\theta_{B}{\mathbf{B}}^{-1}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{B}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}\right){\mathbf{B}}^{-1}\theta_{B}\right)+\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{V}}}^{r}\left({\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{V}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}],\theta_{V}\right)\\
=&\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top})[\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{B}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top},\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{B}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top}]+2\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}),\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top}\rangle\\
&+\left\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta},{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}){\mathbf{B}}+{\mathbf{B}}{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\theta_{V}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U})-2\theta_{U}^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}\right\rangle/2\\
&+\left\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta},{\mathbf{B}}{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\theta_{V}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\theta_{V})+{\rm Sym}({\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\theta_{V}){\mathbf{B}}-2{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top}\theta_{V}+2\theta_{B}{\mathbf{B}}^{-1}\theta_{B}\right\rangle/2\\
&+\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime},2\theta_{B}-{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{B}}-\theta_{U}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{B}}/2-{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\theta_{V}{\mathbf{B}}/2\rangle+\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime\prime},2\theta_{B}-{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{V}^{\top}{\mathbf{V}}-{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}\theta_{V}/2-{\mathbf{B}}\theta_{U}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}/2\rangle.\end{split}

Step 5.
For convenience, we denote
\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{U}}}^{r}(\eta_{U},\theta_{U})={\rm tr}({\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}\eta_{U}^{\top}\theta_{U})
as the
{\mathbf{U}}
part inner product of
\bar{g}^{r}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}
and
\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{Y}}}^{r}(\eta_{Y},\theta_{Y})={\rm tr}({\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}\eta_{Y}^{\top}\theta_{Y})
as the
{\mathbf{Y}}
part inner product of
\bar{g}^{r}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}
. Then

\begin{split}&\quad\overline{{\rm Hess}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})},\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}]\\
&=\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}^{r}\left(\overline{{\rm Hess}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])[\theta_{[{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}]}]},\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}\right)\\
&\overset{\eqref{eq: quotient-hessian-linear-from},\text{Lemma }\ref {lm: quotient-hessian-riemannian-connection-equal}}{=}\bar{g}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}^{r}\left(\widebar{\nabla}_{\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}}\overline{{\rm grad}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])},\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}\right)\\
&\overset{\eqref{eq: koszul-formula-simple-manifolds}}{=}\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{U}}}^{r}\left({\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}],\theta_{U}\right)+\bar{g}_{{\mathbf{Y}}}^{r}\left({\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{Y}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}],\theta_{Y}\right)\\
&\quad+{\rm tr}\left({\rm D}{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}\left[\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{Y}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}\right]\theta_{U}^{\top}\theta_{U}\right)/2+{\rm tr}\left({\rm D}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}\left[\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{Y}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}\right]\theta_{Y}^{\top}\theta_{Y}\right)/2.\end{split}

(111)

Next, we compute
{\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}]
and
{\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{Y}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}]
separately. We have

\begin{split}&{\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{U}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}]\\
=&\lim_{t\to 0}\left(({\mathbf{I}}_{p_{1}}-P_{{\mathbf{U}}+t\theta_{U}})\nabla f\left(({\mathbf{U}}+t\theta_{U})({\mathbf{Y}}+t\theta_{Y})^{\top}\right)({\mathbf{Y}}+t\theta_{Y}){\mathbf{V}}^{-1}_{{\mathbf{Y}}+t\theta_{Y}}-P_{{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{V}}^{-1}_{\mathbf{Y}}\right)/t\\
=&P_{{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}}\left(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top})\theta_{Y}{\mathbf{V}}^{-1}_{\mathbf{Y}}+\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}}{\rm D}{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}[\theta_{Y}]+\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top})[{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{Y}^{\top}+\theta_{U}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}]{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{V}}^{-1}_{\mathbf{Y}}\right)\\
&-({\mathbf{U}}\theta_{U}^{\top}+\theta_{U}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top})\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{V}}^{-1}_{\mathbf{Y}},\end{split}

(112)

and

\begin{split}&{\rm D}\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{Y}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}]\\
=&\lim_{t\to 0}\left((\nabla f\left(({\mathbf{U}}+t\theta_{U})({\mathbf{Y}}+t\theta_{Y})^{\top}\right))^{\top}({\mathbf{U}}+t\theta_{U}){\mathbf{W}}_{({\mathbf{Y}}+t\theta_{Y})}^{-1}-(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}\right)/t\\
=&(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}))^{\top}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}+(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\rm D}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}[\theta_{Y}]+\left(\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top})[{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{Y}^{\top}+\theta_{U}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}]\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}.\end{split}

(113)

By plugging (
112
) and (
113
) into (
111
) and observing the fact
\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}\in\mathcal{H}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r}^{q_{3}}
, we have

\begin{split}&\overline{{\rm Hess}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})},\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}]\\
=&\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top})[{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{Y}^{\top}+\theta_{U}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top},{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{Y}^{\top}+\theta_{U}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}]+2\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}),\theta_{U}\theta_{Y}^{\top}\rangle-\langle{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}},\theta_{U}^{\top}\theta_{U}\rangle\\
&+\langle(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\rm D}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}[\theta_{Y}],\theta_{Y}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}\rangle+\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}}{\rm D}{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}[\theta_{Y}],\theta_{U}{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}\rangle\\
&+{\rm tr}\left({\rm D}{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}\left[\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{Y}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}\right]\theta_{U}^{\top}\theta_{U}\right)/2+\langle{\rm D}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}\left[\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{Y}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}\right],\theta_{Y}^{\top}\theta_{Y}\rangle/2.\end{split}

This finishes the proof of this proposition.
\blacksquare

E.2
Proof of Theorem
7
.

First, recall
{\mathbf{X}}={\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}
and
{\mathbf{V}}
spans the right singular subspace of
{\mathbf{X}}
, so
{\mathbf{Y}}
lies in the column space of
{\mathbf{V}}
and
{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\dagger}=P_{\mathbf{V}}
. Thus, (
69
) is by direct calculation from the gradient expressions in Proposition
4
.

Next, we prove (
70
). Since
[{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}]
is a Riemannian FOSP of (
40c
), we have

\overline{{\rm grad}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}={\mathbf{0}}\quad,\quad(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}={\mathbf{0}}\quad,\text{ and }\quad\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}}={\mathbf{0}}

(114)

and have
{\rm grad}f({\mathbf{X}})={\mathbf{0}}
by (
69
). So
\nabla f({\mathbf{X}})=P_{{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}}\nabla f({\mathbf{X}})P_{{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}}
. Let
\boldsymbol{\Sigma}={\mathbf{U}}^{\top}{\mathbf{X}}{\mathbf{V}}
. Given any
\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}=[\theta_{U}^{\top}\quad\theta_{Y}^{\top}]^{\top}\in\mathcal{H}_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r}^{q_{3}}
, we have

\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{X}}),P_{{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}{\mathbf{V}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1}\theta_{Y}^{\top}P_{{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}}\rangle=\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{X}}),\theta_{U}\theta_{Y}^{\top}\rangle,

(115)

where the equality is because
{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}{\mathbf{V}}={\mathbf{U}}^{\top}{\mathbf{X}}{\mathbf{V}}=\boldsymbol{\Sigma}
and
\nabla f({\mathbf{X}})=P_{{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}}\nabla f({\mathbf{X}})P_{{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}}
.

Then by Proposition
4
:

\begin{split}&\quad\overline{{\rm Hess}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})},\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}]\\
=&\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top})[{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{Y}^{\top}+\theta_{U}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top},{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{Y}^{\top}+\theta_{U}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}]+2\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}),\theta_{U}\theta_{Y}^{\top}\rangle-\langle{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}},\theta_{U}^{\top}\theta_{U}\rangle\\
&+\langle(\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}))^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}{\rm D}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}[\theta_{Y}],\theta_{Y}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}\rangle+\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}){\mathbf{Y}}{\rm D}{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1}[\theta_{Y}],\theta_{U}{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}\rangle\\
&+\langle{\rm D}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}\left[\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{Y}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}\right],\theta_{Y}^{\top}\theta_{Y}\rangle/2+\langle{\rm D}{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}\left[\overline{{\rm grad}_{\mathbf{Y}}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}\right],\theta_{U}^{\top}\theta_{U}\rangle/2\\
&\overset{\eqref{eq: FOSP-condition-general3}}{=}\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top})[{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{Y}^{\top}+\theta_{U}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top},{\mathbf{U}}\theta_{Y}^{\top}+\theta_{U}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}]+2\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}),\theta_{U}\theta_{Y}^{\top}\rangle\\
&\overset{\text{Proposition }\ref {prop: general-bijection3},\eqref{eq: Hessian-con-gradient-general3}}{=}\nabla^{2}f({\mathbf{X}})[\mathcal{L}^{r}_{{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}}(\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}),\mathcal{L}^{r}_{{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}}(\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})})]+2\langle\nabla f({\mathbf{X}}),P_{{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}}\theta_{U}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}{\mathbf{V}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1}\theta_{Y}^{\top}P_{{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}}\rangle\\
&={\rm Hess}f({\mathbf{X}})[\mathcal{L}^{r}_{{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}}(\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}),\mathcal{L}^{r}_{{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}}(\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})})],\end{split}

where the last equality follows from the expression of
{\rm Hess}f({\mathbf{X}})
in (
41
) and the definition of
\mathcal{L}^{r}_{{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}}
.

Then, by (
58
), (
70
) and Theorem
2
, we have
\overline{{\rm Hess}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}])}
has
(p_{1}+p_{2}-r)r
eigenvalues and
\widebar{\lambda}_{i}({\rm Hess}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}]))
is sandwiched between
(\sigma_{r}({\mathbf{W}}^{-1}_{\mathbf{Y}})\wedge\sigma_{r}^{2}({\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1/2}))\lambda_{i}({\rm Hess}f({\mathbf{X}}))
and
(\sigma_{1}({\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1})\vee\sigma^{2}_{1}({\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{V}}_{\mathbf{Y}}^{-1/2}))\lambda_{i}({\rm Hess}f({\mathbf{X}}))
for
i=1,\ldots,(p_{1}+p_{2}-r)r
.
\blacksquare

E.3
Proof of Corollary
2
.

Here we prove the Riemannian FOSP, SOSP, and strict saddle equivalence of (
2
) on
{\cal M}_{r}^{e}
and
{\cal M}_{r}^{q_{1}}
, similar proof applies to the equivalence on
{\cal M}_{r}^{e}
and
{\cal M}_{r}^{q_{2}}
(or
{\cal M}_{r}^{q_{3}}
). First, by the connection of Riemannian gradients in (
60
), the connection of Riemannian FOSPs under two geometries clearly holds.

Suppose
[{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}]
is a Riemannian SOSP of (
40a
) and let
{\mathbf{X}}={\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}
. Given any
\xi_{\mathbf{X}}\in T_{{\mathbf{X}}}{\cal M}^{e}_{r}
, we have
{\rm Hess}f({\mathbf{X}})[\xi_{\mathbf{X}},\xi_{\mathbf{X}}]\overset{\eqref{eq: Hessian-connection-general1}}{=}\overline{{\rm Hess}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}[(\mathcal{L}^{r}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}})^{-1}(\xi_{\mathbf{X}}),(\mathcal{L}^{r}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}})^{-1}(\xi_{\mathbf{X}})]\geq 0
, where the inequality is by the SOSP assumption on
[{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}]
. Combining the fact
{\mathbf{X}}
is a Riemannian FOSP, this shows
{\mathbf{X}}={\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}
is a Riemannian SOSP under the embedded geometry.

Next, let us show the other direction: suppose
{\mathbf{X}}
is a Riemannian SOSP under the embedded geometry, then there is a unique
[{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}]
such that
{\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}={\mathbf{X}}
and it is a Riemannian SOSP of (
40a
). To see this, first the uniqueness of
[{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}]
is guaranteed by the fact
\bar{\ell}:({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})\in\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r}^{q_{1}}\to{\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{R}}^{\top}\in{\cal M}_{r}^{e}
induces a diffeomorphism between
{\cal M}_{r}^{q_{1}}
and
{\cal M}_{r}^{e}
. In addition, we have shown
[{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}]
is a Riemannian FOSP of (
40a
). Then by (
61
), we have for any
\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}\in\mathcal{H}_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r}^{q_{1}}
,
\overline{{\rm Hess}\,h_{r}([{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}])}[\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})},\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}]={\rm Hess}f({\mathbf{X}})[\mathcal{L}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}^{r}(\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}),\mathcal{L}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}^{r}(\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})})]\geq 0
.

Finally, the strict saddle equivalence follows from the sandwich inequality in Theorem
5
and the definition of the strict saddle.

\blacksquare

Appendix F
Additional Lemmas

The following lemma provides the connection of horizontal lifts of tangent vectors at different reference points for the quotient geometries we consider.

Lemma 11
.

(i)

Suppose
\theta_{[{\mathbf{Y}}]}
is a tangent vector to the quotient manifold
{\cal M}_{r+}^{q_{1}}
at
[{\mathbf{Y}}]
. If
{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}={\mathbf{O}}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}}{\mathbf{O}}^{\top}
holds for any
{\mathbf{O}}\in\mathbb{O}_{r}
, then the horizontal lifts of
\theta_{[{\mathbf{Y}}]}
at
{\mathbf{Y}}
and
{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}
for
{\mathbf{O}}\in\mathbb{O}_{r}
are related as
\theta_{{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}}=\theta_{{\mathbf{Y}}}{\mathbf{O}}
.

(ii)

Suppose
\theta_{[{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}}]}
is a tangent vector to the quotient manifold
{\cal M}_{r+}^{q_{2}}
at
[{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}}]
. The horizontal lifts of
\theta_{[{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}}]}
at
({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}})
and at
({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{O}},{\mathbf{O}}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{O}})
are related as
\theta_{({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{O}},{\mathbf{O}}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{O}})}=[(\theta_{U}{\mathbf{O}})^{\top}\quad({\mathbf{O}}^{\top}\theta_{B}{\mathbf{O}})^{\top}]^{\top}
given
\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}})}=[\theta_{U}^{\top}\quad\theta_{B}^{\top}]^{\top}
.

(iii)

Suppose
\theta_{[{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}]}
is a tangent vector to the quotient manifold
{\cal M}_{r}^{q_{1}}
at
[{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}]
. If
{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}={\mathbf{M}}{\mathbf{W}}_{{\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{M}},{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{M}}^{-\top}}{\mathbf{M}}^{\top}
and
{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}}={\mathbf{M}}^{-\top}{\mathbf{V}}_{{\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{M}},{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{M}}^{-\top}}{\mathbf{M}}^{-1}
hold for any
{\mathbf{M}}\in{\rm GL}(r)
, then the horizontal lifts of
\theta_{[{\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}}]}
at
({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})
and at
({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{M}},{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{M}}^{-\top})
are related as
\theta_{({\mathbf{L}}{\mathbf{M}},{\mathbf{R}}{\mathbf{M}}^{-\top})}=[(\theta_{L}{\mathbf{M}})^{\top}\quad(\theta_{R}{\mathbf{M}}^{-\top})^{\top}]^{\top}
given
\theta_{({\mathbf{L}},{\mathbf{R}})}=[\theta_{L}^{\top}\quad\theta_{R}^{\top}]^{\top}
.

(iv)

Suppose
\theta_{[{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}]}
is a tangent vector to the quotient manifold
{\cal M}_{r}^{q_{2}}
at
[{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}]
. The horizontal lifts of
\theta_{[{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}}]}
at
({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})
and at
({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{O}},{\mathbf{O}}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{O}},{\mathbf{V}}{\mathbf{O}})
are related as
\theta_{({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{O}},{\mathbf{O}}^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}{\mathbf{O}},{\mathbf{V}}{\mathbf{O}})}=[(\theta_{U}{\mathbf{O}})^{\top}\quad({\mathbf{O}}^{\top}\theta_{B}{\mathbf{O}})^{\top}\quad(\theta_{V}{\mathbf{O}})^{\top}]^{\top}
given
\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{B}},{\mathbf{V}})}=[\theta_{U}^{\top}\quad\theta_{B}^{\top}\quad\theta_{V}^{\top}]^{\top}
.

(v)

Suppose
\theta_{[{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}]}
is a tangent vector to the quotient manifold
{\cal M}_{r}^{q_{3}}
at
[{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}]
. The horizontal lifts of
\theta_{[{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}}]}
at
({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})
and at
({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{O}},{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}})
are related as
\theta_{({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{O}},{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}})}=[(\theta_{U}{\mathbf{O}})^{\top}\quad(\theta_{Y}{\mathbf{O}})^{\top}]^{\top}
given
\theta_{({\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{Y}})}=[\theta_{U}^{\top}\quad\theta_{Y}^{\top}]^{\top}
.

Proof of Lemma
11
.
Since the proofs for claims (ii)(iii)(iv)(v) are similar to the proof of the first claim, for simplicity, we only present the proof for the first one. Let
l:{\cal M}_{r+}^{q_{1}}\to\mathbb{R}
be an arbitrary smooth function and define
\bar{l}:=l\circ\pi:\widebar{{\cal M}}^{q_{1}}_{r+}\to\mathbb{R}
, where
\pi
is the quotient mapping on the manifold
{\cal M}_{r+}^{q_{1}}
. Let
z:{\mathbf{Y}}\to{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}
, so we have

\bar{l}({\mathbf{Y}})=\bar{l}(z({\mathbf{Y}})),\quad\forall{\mathbf{Y}}\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times r}_{*}.

(116)

By taking differential with respect to
{\mathbf{Y}}
along direction
\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}\in\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{Y}}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r+}^{q_{1}}
on both sides of (
116
), we have

{\rm D}\bar{l}({\mathbf{Y}})[\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}]={\rm D}\bar{l}(z({\mathbf{Y}}))[{\rm D}z({\mathbf{Y}})[\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}]].

(117)

Moreover, by chain rule and the definition of the horizontal lift of a tangent vector, we have

{\rm D}\bar{l}({\mathbf{Y}})[\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}]={\rm D}l(\pi({\mathbf{Y}}))[{\rm D}\pi({\mathbf{Y}})[\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}]]={\rm D}l(\pi({\mathbf{Y}}))[\theta_{[{\mathbf{Y}}]}].

(118)

In addition,

{\rm D}z({\mathbf{Y}})[\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}]=\lim_{t\to 0}(z({\mathbf{Y}}+t\theta_{\mathbf{Y}})-z({\mathbf{Y}}))/t=\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}.

(119)

Then,

\begin{split}{\rm D}l(\pi({\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}))[\theta_{[{\mathbf{Y}}]}]={\rm D}l(\pi({\mathbf{Y}}))[\theta_{[{\mathbf{Y}}]}]\overset{\eqref{eq: horizonal-lift-connect-eq3}}{=}{\rm D}\bar{l}({\mathbf{Y}})[\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}]&\overset{\eqref{eq: horizonal-lift-connect-eq2}}{=}{\rm D}\bar{l}(z({\mathbf{Y}}))[{\rm D}z({\mathbf{Y}})[\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}]]\\
&\overset{\eqref{eq: horizonal-lift-connect-eq4}}{=}{\rm D}\bar{l}(z({\mathbf{Y}}))[\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}]\\
&\overset{(a)}{=}{\rm D}l(\pi({\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}))[{\rm D}\pi({\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}})[\theta_{{\mathbf{Y}}}{\mathbf{O}}]].\end{split}

Here (a) is by chain rule and the definition of
z
. Since the above equation holds for any
l
, this implies
{\rm D}\pi({\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}})[\theta_{{\mathbf{Y}}}{\mathbf{O}}]=\theta_{[{\mathbf{Y}}]}
. Finally, by Lemma
4
(i), we have
\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{Y}}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r+}^{q_{1}}=\{\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}^{\prime}:\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}^{\prime}=({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{S}}+{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}{\mathbf{D}}){\mathbf{P}}^{-\top},{\mathbf{D}}\in\mathbb{R}^{(p-r)\times r},{\mathbf{S}}{\mathbf{P}}^{-\top}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{P}}^{-1}\in\mathbb{S}^{r\times r}\}
, where
{\mathbf{U}}\in{\rm St}(r,p)
spans the top
r
eigenspace of
{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{Y}}^{\top}
and
{\mathbf{P}}={\mathbf{U}}^{\top}{\mathbf{Y}}
. Then, it holds that
\mathcal{H}_{{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r+}^{q_{1}}=\{\theta_{{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}}^{\prime}:\theta_{{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}}^{\prime}=({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{S}}+{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}{\mathbf{D}}){\mathbf{P}}^{-\top}{\mathbf{O}},{\mathbf{D}}\in\mathbb{R}^{(p-r)\times r},{\mathbf{S}}{\mathbf{P}}^{-\top}{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{P}}^{-1}\in\mathbb{S}^{r\times r}\}
. Thus, we have
\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}\in\mathcal{H}_{{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r+}^{q_{1}}
given
\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}\in\mathcal{H}_{{\mathbf{Y}}}\widebar{{\cal M}}_{r+}^{q_{1}}
and conclude
\theta_{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}
is the unique horizontal lift of
\theta_{[{\mathbf{Y}}]}
at
{\mathbf{Y}}{\mathbf{O}}
.

\blacksquare

Lemma 12
.

Suppose
f:{\cal M}\to\mathbb{R}
is a smooth function defined on the Riemannian quotient manifold
{\cal M}=\widebar{{\cal M}}/\sim
and
\widebar{{\cal M}}
is endowed with the Riemannian metric
\bar{g}_{\mathbf{X}}
, which induces a Riemannian metric
g_{[{\mathbf{X}}]}
on
{\cal M}
. Then
\overline{{\rm Hess}f([{\mathbf{X}}])}[\theta_{[{\mathbf{X}}]},\eta_{[{\mathbf{X}}]}]=\bar{g}_{\mathbf{X}}\left(\widebar{\nabla}_{\theta_{\mathbf{X}}}\overline{{\rm grad}f},\eta_{\mathbf{X}}\right)
for any
\theta_{[{\mathbf{X}}]},\eta_{[{\mathbf{X}}]}\in T_{[{\mathbf{X}}]}{\cal M}
.

Proof of Lemma
12
.
Suppose the vertical and horizontal spaces on
\widebar{{\cal M}}
are
\mathcal{V}_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}
and
\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}
with
\mathcal{V}_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}\oplus\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}=T_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}
. Let us define
\mathcal{V}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}
to be the subspace in
T_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}
that is orthogonal to
\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}
with respect to
\bar{g}_{\mathbf{X}}
. In general,
\mathcal{V}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}
is not equal to
\mathcal{V}_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}
unless we pick the horizontal space in a canonical way.

Since
\widebar{\nabla}_{\theta_{\mathbf{X}}}\overline{{\rm grad}f}
by definition belongs to
T_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}
, we have for
\theta_{\mathbf{X}},\eta_{\mathbf{X}}\in\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}
,

\begin{split}\overline{{\rm Hess}f([{\mathbf{X}}])}[\theta_{[{\mathbf{X}}]},\eta_{[{\mathbf{X}}]}]&=\bar{g}_{\mathbf{X}}\left(\overline{{\rm Hess}f([{\mathbf{X}}])[\theta_{[{\mathbf{X}}]}]},\eta_{\mathbf{X}}\right)\\
&\overset{\eqref{eq: quotient-hessian-linear-from}}{=}\bar{g}_{\mathbf{X}}\left(P_{\mathbf{X}}^{\mathcal{H}}\left(\widebar{\nabla}_{\theta_{\mathbf{X}}}\overline{{\rm grad}f}\right),\eta_{\mathbf{X}}\right)\\
&=\bar{g}_{\mathbf{X}}\left(\widebar{\nabla}_{\theta_{\mathbf{X}}}\overline{{\rm grad}f}-P_{\mathbf{X}}^{\mathcal{V}^{\prime}}\left(\widebar{\nabla}_{\theta_{\mathbf{X}}}\overline{{\rm grad}f}\right),\eta_{\mathbf{X}}\right)\\
&\overset{(a)}{=}\bar{g}_{\mathbf{X}}\left(\widebar{\nabla}_{\theta_{\mathbf{X}}}\overline{{\rm grad}f},\eta_{\mathbf{X}}\right),\end{split}

where (a) is because
\eta_{\mathbf{X}}\in\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}
and
P_{\mathbf{X}}^{\mathcal{H}}
and
P_{\mathbf{X}}^{\mathcal{V}^{\prime}}
denote the projection operators onto
\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}
and
\mathcal{V}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{X}}\widebar{{\cal M}}
, respectively.
\blacksquare

Lemma 13
.

(Max-min Theorem for Eigenvalues
[
Bha13
, Corollary III.1.2]
)
Suppose
{\mathbf{A}}
is a Hermitian operator on the Hilbert space
\mathcal{H}
with dimension
p
and inner product
g(\cdot,\cdot)
and
{\mathbf{A}}
has eigenvalues
\lambda_{1}\geq\lambda_{2}\geq\cdots\geq\lambda_{p}
. If
{\mathscr{C}}_{k}
denotes the set of
k
-dimensional subspaces of
\mathcal{H}
, then
\lambda_{k}=\max_{C\in{\mathscr{C}}_{k}}\min_{{\mathbf{u}}\in C,{\mathbf{u}}\neq 0}g({\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{A}}{\mathbf{u}})/g({\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{u}}).
